欧州大規模デモが問う財政再建の道 
金融市場が動揺するリスクは当面残存する見通し

○ このところ欧州ではインフレに伴う生活苦や公共サービスの劣化等を背景に各地で大規模抗議活
動が発生している 
○ 大規模デモは緊縮を伴う財政健全化を難しくする要因となる。財政懸念や政情不安は国債利回り
の上昇やユーロ安を招いており、経済への悪影響も懸念される 
○ 来年にかけて、欧州では複数の重要な国政選挙が予定される中、政治・財政を巡る不安が欧州金
融市場を動揺させるリスクは当面残存するだろう

１．欧州で広がる大規模抗議活動 
このところ、欧州では大規模デモが多発している（図表1）。フランスでは、9月以降、教員不足や
学校施設の不備など、教育環境の改善を求める高校生の抗議活動が続いていた。こうした中で、10月
半ばには、物価高への対策や公共サービスの改善を求める市民も加わり、抗議活動は全国規模に拡大
した。 
大規模デモが発生しているのはフランスだけではない。フランスのデモに一部触発される形でベル
図表 1 9 月以降に欧州で発生した主な抗議活動 図表2 EU：生活苦の割合（左）と 
自国が抱える課題（右）

### Page 2

2

ギーやイタリア等でも抗議活動が発生している。また、スペインでは、高齢女性が家賃を支払えずに
強制退去させられたことをきっかけに、住宅不足や住宅価格・家賃高騰等に対する不満が爆発する形
で大規模な抗議活動が発生し、これが遠因となりサンチェス首相は議会の解散に追い込まれた。 
各地で発生する大規模抗議活動の背景には、近年のインフレによる生活苦やこれを受けた政府への
不満がある1。EUにおいて、生活苦を訴える人の割合は低中所得層を中心に2022年以降高まっている
ほか、 自国が直面する課題としてインフレや経済状況を挙げる人の割合も高い （図表2）。これらの要
因が、自国政府に対する信頼感の低迷につながっている（図表3）。 
本稿では、生活改善を求めて各国で広がる抗議活動が経済や財政、金融市場に与える影響について
考察する。 
２．大規模抗議活動は財政健全化を阻む要因に 
一連の抗議活動は、2018年にフランスで発生した黄色いベスト運動を想起させる2。 当時の参加者は、
自動車に常備が義務付けられている黄色い安全ベストを着用し、燃料税の増税や生活費高騰に抗議し
た。購買力の低下や政府への不満が背景にある点は当時と共通している。異なるのは、政府の追加的
な財政支出の余地が、当時と比べてさらに狭まっていることである。 
欧州債務危機が起きた2010年代前半以降、順調に進捗してきた政府債務の削減は、2020年代に入る
と足踏みしはじめた。パンデミック時の大規模給付やウクライナ戦争後のエネルギー高対策など、危
機対応の財政出動が頻発したためだ。黄色いベスト運動が発生した2018年と比べて政府債務が増加し
たEU加盟国は、27カ国中18カ国にのぼる（図表4）。 
加えて、足元では防衛費増額や社会保障費・利払い負担の拡大など、構造的な財政支出圧力も高ま
っている。 各国の次年度予算案を踏まえると、 財政赤字は当面、高止まりする公算が大きい （図表5） 。
安全保障環境の変化やエネルギー価格の高騰を踏まえ、EUは一定の防衛費・エネルギー関連の追加支
出をEU財政ルールの対象外とする柔軟化措置を設けている。 しかし、 当該措置を適用しているドイツ
図表3 EU市民の自国政府への信頼度 図表4 EU加盟国の一般政府債務残高の変化 
（2018年～2025年）

### Page 3

3

やベルギーでは、特例対象の追加支出を除いてもなお、財政赤字をGDP比3％に抑制するEUの基準を
超過する見込みである。それでもベルギーでは、連立与党間の予算協議が決裂し、首相が辞意を表明
する事態に陥った。 
フランスを起点に広がった抗議活動は、 緊縮を伴う財政健全化の難しさを浮き彫りにしている。2018
年の黄色いベスト運動では、マクロン政権が燃料税増税の撤回や所得支援策の拡充に踏み切ったこと
で、抗議活動が漸く沈静化に向かった。今回は、11月に新たなデモやストライキが計画されるなど、
いまだ抗議活動が収束する見通しは立っていない。各国政府は事態の長期化を回避するため、家計支
援や公共サービス拡充などの対応を迫られる可能性があり、財政拡張圧力が一段と強まることが懸念
される。 
３．政治・財政を巡る不安が欧州金融市場を動揺させるリスクは当面残存 
金融市場は、欧州主要国の財政状況に対する警戒を強めている。 
フランスをはじめとする高債務国では、政治や財政を巡る混乱を嫌気して、10年債利回りが上昇し
ている（図表6）。2010年代の欧州債務危機を受けてECB（欧州中央銀行）は、条件付きで対象国の国
債を無制限に購入可能なOMT（Outright Monetary Transactions）や経済の基礎的条件では正当化されな
い国債市場の混乱が金融政策の波及を阻害する場合に発動可能なTPI （Transmission Protection 
Instrument）などの市場安定化措置3を整備してきた。当時よりセーフティーネットが整備されている
こともあり、足元の国債利回り上昇が欧州債務危機時のような金融危機につながる可能性は現時点で
は低い。 
とはいえ、金利の上昇は景気下押しや、さらなる財政悪化の要因となる。金利上昇幅が大きいフラ
ンスを例に足元の利回り上昇幅を踏まえて試算すると、設備投資・住宅投資が1年後に3％程度減少す
るとの結果が得られる （図表7） 。金利上昇やそれに伴う景気減速は、利払い費の増加や税収の減少を
図表5 主要国の財政収支実績と見通し 図表6 ユーロ圏の高債務国と 
ドイツの10年債利回り

きなかった国。数値は成立前の政府予算案ベース。

### Page 4

4

通じて、財政をさらに悪化させる要因にもなる。 
各国の政情不安や財政への懸念は、ECBの金融政策運営をも左右する可能性がある。足元では、ユ
ーロが主要通貨に対して下落している （図表8） 。 ユーロ安は、 輸入物価の上昇を通じてインフレ率を
押し上げる要因となる。足元のユーロ下落幅を前提に、時系列モデルを用いてユーロ圏のインフレ率
への影響を試算すると、総合インフレ率は0.3％Pt程度、コアインフレ率は0.1％Pt程度押し上げられる
との結果が得られた（図表9）。 ユーロ圏では、イラン情勢の緊迫化に伴うエネルギー価格の高騰を背
景に、直近でもインフレ率が加速している。先行きユーロ安圧力が強い状況が続けば、インフレ率に
一段の上昇圧力がかかる可能性がある。しかし、ECBはインフレ圧力が一段と強まっても、状況によ
っては追加利上げに踏み切りにくくなることが想定される。利上げがさらなる長期金利の上昇、国債
市場の不安定化を招くおそれがあるためだ。このように政情不安や財政懸念は、ECBの金融政策運営
を困難にする要因にもなりかねない。 
各国の財政や政治情勢に対する金融市場の懸念は当面残存する可能性が高い。欧州では、今年11月
にスペイン議会選挙、来年にはフランス大統領選挙やイタリア議会選挙など、重要な国政選挙が相次
いで予定されている。大規模デモの発生に加えて選挙も控える中、各国政府は国民に負担を求める緊
縮的な財政政策を例年以上に打ち出しにくくなっている。また、各国の選挙では極右政党やポピュリ
スト政党の勢力拡大が予想され、選挙後の政治はより一層不安定化するリスクがある。このほか、コ
ロナ禍からの経済復興を目的に設立されたEU復興基金の受け取りが終了することも、 来年以降の財政
懸念を強める要因になるだろう。同基金の配分が手厚かった南欧や中東欧諸国は、自国の財政負担を
抑えながら公共投資などを通じて経済成長を支えてきたが、来年以降はこうした効果が徐々に剥落す
ることになる。

### Page 5

5

以上のように、2020年代に入ってから、コロナ禍におけるサプライチェーン混乱、ロシアのウクラ
イナ侵攻やイラン情勢の緊迫によるエネルギー高が立て続けに起きてインフレが継続する中、欧州各
国で有権者の生活コスト上昇に対する不満が強まっている。一方、これらの緊急事態に対応するため
財政出動を繰り返してきたことに加え、高齢化に伴う社会保障費の増加、地政学リスクの高まりを背
景とした防衛費増強の必要性、利払い費の増大に直面しており、各国の財政余力は著しく低下してい
る。来年にかけて予定されている主要国の選挙では、極右・ポピュリスト政党の勢力拡大が予想され
ており、政治の混乱に拍車がかかる懸念もある。欧州の政治情勢や財政状況に対する不安が来年にか
けて沈静化するとは考えにくく、国債市場や為替市場が動揺するリスクに引き続き留意する必要があ
ろう。

Document 069: 2026年10月贸易数据点评：外贸增长的价格权重持续上升

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026年10月贸易数据点评：外贸增长的价格权重持续上升.pdf
Report source: 西南证券
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

请务必阅读正文后的重要声明部分  
1 
ooo[Table_ReportInfo] 2026 年 11 月 10 日      
证 券研究报告 •宏观简评报告      
数据点评  
外贸增长的价格权重 持续 上升 
——2026 年 10 月贸易数据点评

点评

西 南证券研究院  
[Table_Summary]  摘要：从贸易总体情况看，以美元计，2026 年 1—10 月我国货物贸易进出口
总额同比增长 25.3%，较 1—9 月扩大 1.5 个百分点。10 月单月出口同比增长
35.2%， 进口同比增长41.6%， 分别较上月回落1.1个百分点、 加快10.6 个百
分点， 进口增速创2021年 6 月以来新高， 贸易顺差收窄至1052.8亿美元。 展
望后续，去年同期低基数及主要经济体制造业PMI 仍处扩张区间，有望支撑
11 月外贸增速维持较高水平， 但前期集中出货效应减弱、 海外需求波动及贸易
政策不确定性或带来扰动。从贸易伙伴看，10 月我国对主要贸易伙伴出口增
速普遍回落，但对韩国出口同比增长92.4%，较上月加快 24.3 个百分点；自
韩国进口同比增长 110.2%，较上月加快15.9个百分点。对韩贸易高增主要受
AI 产业链涨价及加工贸易重复计算效应共同推动，若后续AI 硬件价格见顶回
落，对韩名义贸易增速或随之放缓。从出口结构看，机电产品、高新技术产品
出口金额同比分别增长46.1%、66.6%，自动数据处理设备及其零部件、集成
电路出口分别增长 114.8%、137.1%，AI 算力基础设施建设持续提供需求支撑。
不过，集成电路出口数量同比下降 2.5%，金额高增与实际出口数量增长存在
明显背离。汽车及零配件出口金额分别增长37.6%、32.1%，通用机械设备出
口增长 34.5%，出口动能有所扩散；手机、家电等传统消费电子出口增速边际
放缓，劳动密集型产品出口表现相对偏弱。从进口结构看，机电产品、高新技
术产品进口金额同比涨幅分别扩大至57.5%、72.1%，集成电路、自动数据处
理设备及其零部件进口分别增长 78.3%、261.0%，但集成电路进口数量同比
持平，反映 AI 产业链进口增长尚未同步体现为实物数量扩张。资源品方面，
原油、 成品油进口金额分别增长41.0%、45.2%， 但进口数量仍分别下降13.3%、
10.2%；铁矿石进口金额同比下降11.5%，铜及铜材进口金额增长45.6%，资
源品进口表现分化。汽车进口金额和数量分别下降38.5%、43.1%，农产品及
粮食、大豆进口也有所收缩。整体来看，10 月外贸增长仍主要由 AI 产业链及
部分资源品价格上涨支撑，出口结构向电子产业链集中，进口则呈现高技术产
品偏强、传统资源品及汽车偏弱的分化特征，剔除价格因素后，实际贸易需求
仍有待进一步改善。 
 进口超预期提速，顺差收窄。按美元计，2026年 1-10 月，我国货物贸易进出
口总额同比增长 25.3%，涨幅相比 1‑ 9 月扩大 1.5 个百分点，持续保持高位。
10 月单月，货物贸易出口同比增长 35.2%，较上月回落 1.1 个百分点，高于
wind 一致预测值（33.25%）；进口同比增长41.6%，较上月回升 10.6 个百分
点，显著高于 wind 一致预测值（30.36%），创 2021年 6 月以来最快同比增
速。贸易顺差为 1052.8 亿美元， 虽高于去年同期， 低于wind 一致预测值 （1195
亿美元），为 5 月以来最小单月顺差。关税与贸易谈判的不确定性催生企业前
置出货， 是今年出口高增的重要推手之一；不过随着窗口期临近，前期订单已
逐渐兑现，该支撑的边际贡献逐步回落。 同时，近期人民币升值放缓对美元计
价出口的折算贡献有所回落。进口端的高增则主要来自 AI 产业链产品与资源
品涨价，叠加去年同期低基数的驱动。从海外国际景气表现来看，10 月美欧
日制造业景气均有所回落：标普全球美国制造业 PMI 下修至 54.9，新订单增
速降至三月以来最低水平，反映出关税对国际销售的影响。能源、燃料、运输

2
和金属价格上涨叠加关税影响， 推动投入成本通胀升至五月以来最高水平；欧
元区制造业 PMI 终值回落至 52.7，产出增速放缓至四个月低点，但整体仍保
持稳健；日本制造业 PMI 终值回落至 53.3，企业产出和新订单增幅均放缓，
且增长速度都降至七个月来最低。贸易政策方面，11 月 9 日国务院关税税则
委员会发布公告，宣布自 11 月 10 日起 2 个月内继续暂停 24%的对美加征关
税、保留 10%税率。此次展期安排有助于稳定外贸企业对美订单与产能预期，
为后续谈判保留政策缓冲期。近期美方同步将 178 项对华 301 关税豁免延期至
2027 年 1 月 9 日，豁免清单以工业中间品、医疗用品、光伏制造设备为主，
有助于降低相关品类对美出口关税成本。往后看，去年四季度基数较低， 主要
经济体制造业 PMI也仍在扩张区间，11月贸易增速仍有望维持在较高水平，
但动能或边际放缓。出口方面，存储芯片价格上涨仍将提供支撑。进口方面，
芯片需求有望继续支撑年底进口增速， 而原油价格环比走弱可能拖累资源品进

口金额。整体来看，AI 硬件仍是影响进出口表现的核心变量，但剔除价格因
素后，真实外需与内需信号或仍相对偏弱。
 单月对韩贸易实现高增，低基数叠加 AI 链涨价共同支撑。以美元计，从出口
总额看，10 月单月我国对主要贸易伙伴出口同比增速普遍边际回落，但是对
韩国出口同比增速边际加快。对美国、东盟出口同比增速分别回落 6.6、5.9
个百分点至 32.9%、46.3%。对韩出口增速加快 24.3 个百分点至 92.4%。从
进口总额看，除欧盟以外，10 月单月我国对主要贸易伙伴的进口同比增速均
加快。对韩国、美国、日本、东盟进口同比增速分别加快 15.9、14.2、11.1、
5.8 个百分点至 110.2%、35.0%、26.5%、42.0%。从韩国前 20 日贸易数据看，
10 月 1-20 日，韩国半导体出口额同比增长222.5%，占出口总额的 44.7%。
本月我国自韩国进口金额高增主要受 AI 产业链产品价格上涨推动，对韩出口
高增则来自加工贸易的重复计算效应。同一批芯片的中间形态在双边贸易统计

中可能被计入了不止一次， 而芯片价格的上涨会同时放大进口额和出口额。 若
后续 AI 硬件价格见顶回落，对韩名义出口增速可能随之回落，且实际贸易量
未必出现同等幅度的收缩。此外，10 月中欧就混动汽车贸易安排达成谅解，
拟设置首年 35万辆的进口配额，次年起配额每年上调6%，措施最早拟于 12
月 1 日生效。该配额机制名义适用于所有来源地混动进口， 因此对欧汽车出口
的边际拖累或在年底至明年初逐步显现。
 出口品类集中度提升，AI 产业链为景气主线。从出口金额看，以美元计，10
月单月机电产品、高新技术产品出口金额同比分别增长46.1%、66.6%，增速
较上月分别加快 2.4、3.0 个百分点， 占出口总额的比重分别为67.42%、33.56%
（两类统计口径存在交叉，部分产品同时归入两类），说明出口高增品类进一
步向电子链条集中。 分产品看，AI 相关电子产品出口表现突出，自动数据处理
设备及其零部件、集成电路出口金额同比分别增长114.8%、137.1%，增速较
上月分别加快 21.8、1.1个百分点。 传统消费电子出口增速则有所放缓， 手机、

家用电器、 音视频设备及其零件出口金额同比分别增长13.2%、20.9%、12.1%，
增速较上月均边际放缓。汽车包括底盘、汽车零配件出口金额同比分别增长
37.6%、32.1%，而包括箱包、服装、鞋靴等品类的传统劳动密集型产品同比
增速均边际回落，显示传统轻工外需承压。通用机械设备出口金额同比上升
34.5%，增速边际加快 19 个百分点，或反映海外制造业资本开支回暖，全球
中游设备需求边际改善。成品油出口金额同比涨幅收窄 44.8 个百分点至
80.3%。从出口数量看，重点电子产品呈现明显量价分化。集成电路出口数量
同比由上月上升 0.9%转为下降 2.5%， 说明本月集成电路出口金额的爆发几乎
完全由单价上涨贡献。 手机出口数量同比下降18.2%，降幅进一步扩大， 出口
金额增长主要受高端机型占比提升和产品单价上涨带动，而出口数量降幅扩
大，则反映全球手机终端需求仍显疲弱，也可能与部分低端组装订单持续向海
外转移有关。 汽车包括底盘、 家用电器出口数量同比分别上升33.5%、14.1%，

但涨幅均较上月收窄，或由于节前出货、赶关税窗口等因素释放后，增速自然
回落。其中，汽车品类出口量价齐升， 但汽车出口数量增速中枢已从二季度的

### Page 3

请务必阅读正文后的重要声明部分
3
极端水平回落， 或反映汽车出口增量逻辑从单纯放量逐步向结构升级、单价抬
升切换。往后看，年内出口结构或延续向电子产业链集中的特征，但需关注价
格上涨与实际出口数量的背离。 传统消费电子需求偏弱， 手机出口数量或继续
承压；汽车出口则受前期集中出货效应消退影响，增速可能进一步回落。
 涨价驱动 AI 链进口高增，资源品进口读数受价格支撑。从进口金额看，以美
元计， 机电产品、 高新技术产品进口金额同比涨幅分别扩大至57.5%、72.1%，
反映进口增长进一步向电子产业链集中。 分产品看，AI 相关电子产品进口保持
高增长，集成电路进口金额同比涨幅小幅扩大至78.3%，自动数据处理设备及
其零部件进口金额同比上升261.0%，涨幅较上月收窄35.5 个百分点。能源资
源品进口金额有所回升， 原油进口金额同比上升41.0%， 涨幅较上月扩大37.7
个百分点，成品油进口金额同比由上月下降转为上升45.2%。铁矿砂及其精矿
进口金额同比下降 11.5%，降幅较上月扩大， 说明黑色系原料的进口价值量有

所收缩； 铜矿砂及其精矿进口金额同比上升23.1%，涨幅较上月收窄，未锻造
的铜及铜材进口金额同比上升45.6%，涨幅较上月扩大。10 月 LME铜期货结
算均价同比上升 35.9%，涨幅较上月收窄约 10 个百分点，但价格同比仍维持
较高正增，或指向铜进口金额高增主要由铜价处于历史高位拉动。 汽车包括底
盘进口金额同比下降38.5%，降幅较上月扩大；农产品、粮食和大豆进口金额
同比均由上月上升转为下降。从进口数量看，电子产品进口呈现价格强、 数量
弱的特征。集成电路进口数量同比持平，说明AI 资本开支周期对价格周期与
订单节奏高度敏感，而实物数量的需求并未同步显著扩张。 能源品进口金额增
长主要受到价格推动。原油、成品油进口数量同比分别下降13.3%、10.2%，
降幅较上月分别收窄8.2、42.9 个百分点。10 月布伦特原油现货均价为125.0
美元/桶，同比上升 92.3%，涨幅较上月扩大近20 个百分点。在原油进口数量
同比仍在下降的背景下， 原油进口金额高增主要由价格弹性主导。 汽车及农产

品进口则延续数量收缩。汽车包括底盘进口数量同比下降43.1%，降幅较上月
收窄 6.9个百分点，本月汽车进口量价两端均明显收缩，而数量端收缩幅度更
大，反映国产车全面替代、进口车市场结构性退潮的长期趋势。粮食和大豆进
口数量同比降幅均有所扩大，本月农产品量价两端均为收缩，反映饲料需求偏
弱，粮食储备节奏偏慢。往后看，集成电路进口金额有望延续较高增长， 但进
口数量增长偏弱， 价格因素仍是重要驱动。资源品方面， 原油价格走弱或削弱
进口金额增速，铜价仍可能提供支撑。整体来看，进口增长仍集中于AI 相关
硬件，传统资源品及消费品进口需求尚未出现全面改善。
 风险提示：国内政策落地不及预期，海外经济波动超预期， 地缘政治风险超预
期。

分析师承诺

投资评级说明  
报告中投资建议所涉及的评级分为公司评级和行业评级（另有说明的除外）。评级标准为报告发布日后 6 个月内
的相对市场表现，即：以报告发布日后 6 个月内公司股价（或行业指数）相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数的涨跌幅
作为基准。其中：A 股市场以沪深 300 指数为基准， 新三板市场以三板成指 （针对协议转让标的） 或三板做市指数 （针
对做市转让标的）为基准；香港市场以恒生指数为基准；美国市场以纳斯达克综合指数或标普 500 指数为基准。  
公司评级

持有：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅介于 10%与 20%之间 
中性：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅介于 -10%与 10%之间  
回避：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅介于 -20%与-10%之间  
卖出：未来 6 个月内，个股相对同期相关证券市场代表性指数涨幅在 -20%以下  
行业评级

跟随大市：未来 6 个月内，行业整体回报介于同期相关证券市场代表性指数 -5%与 5%之间 
弱于大市：未来 6 个月内，行业整体回报低于同期相关证券市场代表性指数 -5%以下

### Page 1

King Yuan Electronics Corp.

Testing business diversification strengthen 
’27 outlook at low-end valuation

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 330.00 TWD | Price: 228.50 TWD

3Q26 results in line; 4Q TPU ramp offsets GPU headwind 
3Q26 sales were +9% QoQ backed by healthy demand from its GPU/CPU customers and 
early mobile inventory builds. With higher depreciation and labor cost moving to COGS 
as new facility production kick off, GM dipped 190bps QoQ to 37.6% in 3Q26 but 
operating leverage lifted OpM 40bps QoQ to 25.8%, taking EPS to NT$2.06. 4Q26 sales 
is guided +7-9% QoQ, in line with our preview, due to slower GPU ramp from continued 
HBM4 yield issue at Hynix partially offsetting strength in AI ASICs (Google’s v8t/i TPUs) 
and CPUs (Nvidia Vera, Google Axion and Microsoft Cobalt). Despite better business mix, 
the rising cost and slower UTR improvement should keep GMs stable at 36-37% in 4Q. 
Solid position into 2027 across chipset and wafer testing 
KYEC’s ’26 sales is now on track to grow 34% YoY and could further accelerate in ’27 at 
+53% YoY from a diversifying application base across GPU, CPU, networking switches and 
ASICs. It is also in a strong position to capture its foundry partner’s chip probing 
outsourcing demand in addition to higher testing unit/dollar content from its existing 
direct customer business. To support expansion across wafer sorting, chip testing and 
system level testing, the company is accelerating its clean room construction in Taiwan 
and has announced it will grow its testing footprint in the US. While its ’27 capex could 
stay at elevated levels, we think the investment mix shifting toward facility should keep its 
depreciation under control. This along with capex-light tool consignment business model 
could drive its GMs to 40%+ in 1H27 and keep profitability resilient through the cycles. 
Recent pullback to low-end valuation: particularly attractive 
We keep EPS for ‘26/27/28E largely unchanged at NT$7.87/NT$15.36/NT$19.18, and 
reiterate our Buy rating with PO of NT$330 still based on 18x 2H27-1H28E P/E (vs. its 
10-25x P/E range). Following the AI sentiment drag, the stock is trading at 15x/11x 
‘27/28E P/E, attractive as the business downside is protected by its leadership in scale 
and execution for AI chipset testing even if AI investment moderates in ‘28.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Founded in 1987 and headquartered in Hsinchu, Taiwan, 
King Yuan Electronics Corp. (KYEC) is a niche Outsourced 
Semiconductor Assembly and Test (OSAT) company 
focused on IC testing services, with market shares of 3% in 
OSAT (IC packaging+testing, number 7 vendor) and 14% in 
IC testing (number 2 vendor) globally. Its capacity is mainly 
located in Greater China. In addition to IC testing, it has 
c.20% revenue exposure to IC packaging, mainly at its 
Chinese subsidiary and mainly for memory products.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on KYEC, in view of undervalued 
upside from smartphone business for both near-term and 
longer-term, structural opportunities in AI areas led by GPU, 
as well as the solid GPM/ROE expansion toward into 2025.

### Page 5

Price objective basis & risk 
King Yuan Electronics Corp. (KYUFF) 
We value KYEC at NT$330 per share, based on 18x 2H27-1H28E P/E, which is close to 
its historical average since 2010. In our view, P/E valuation is appropriate for KYEC given 
its strong growth in earnings over the past few years, future growth potentials with 
upside in total addressable market (TAM) and market share, and enhanced cash position. 
We think 18x P/E, which is above the average P/E level of global OSAT peers, is 
undemanding, in view of its better ROE profile and 2-3% dividend yield.

Downside risks are 
1) faster-than-expected pick-up by testing peers in high-end AI area, 
2) greater-than-expected slowdown in global smartphone/consumer electronics demand, 
3) potential asset disposal caused by strategy shift amid the ongoing decoupling in the 
semis supply chain.

iQmethod SMis the set of BofA Global Research standard measures that serve to maintain global consistency under three broad headings: Business Performance, Quality of Earnings, and validations. The key features of 
iQmethod are: A consistently structured, detailed, and transparent methodology. Guidelines to maximize the effectiveness of the comparative valuation process, and to identify some common pitfalls. 
iQdatabase ®  is our real-time global research database that is sourced directly from our equity analysts’ earnings models and includes forecasted as well as historical data for income statements, balance sheets, and cash 
flow statements for companies covered by BofA Global Research.

### Page 1

Wiwynn

AI-driven sales growth since 4Q; reiterate 
Buy and trim PO to NT$2,310

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 2,310 TWD | Price: 1,555 TWD

3Q beat driven by both general and ASIC servers 
Wiwynn’s 3Q sales grew 56% QoQ/63% YoY, supported by sustained general server 
demand as well as a strong ramp of ASIC servers (e.g., AWS Trainium 3) from Sep. 
GM/OPM of 8.87%/7.47% was above BofAe of 7.77%/6.42% and consensus of 
8.42%/6.85%. OP was 40%/16% above BofAe/consensus. After a non-op. loss (mainly 
from higher interest expenses), NP was 34%/10% above BofAe/consensus.  
4Q sales led by ASIC; GPU sales expected by year-end 
The company posted strong Oct sales of NT$165.1bn (-4% MoM/+81% YoY), tracking 
35%/36% of BofA/consensus estimates. Its general server business was impacted by 
component shortages in Oct, despite demand remaining strong. For AI servers, we 
expect ASIC to be the major sales driver in 4Q with a strong ramp since Sep; while GPU 
servers (from multiple customers) are expected to deliver sales contribution by year-end.  
Solid sales growth in 2027-28; GM pressured by AI  
Our checks indicate that Wiwynn’s ASIC projects remain on a buy-and-sell model, 
although a consignment business model is under discussion; while business models for 
GPU projects have yet to be confirmed. With strong AI server momentum, we expect its 
GM to decline QoQ to 7.67% in 4Q26. For 2027/28, we model solid YoY sales growth of 
+60%/+31% , with conservative GMs of 6.67%/6.51% in 2027/28, vs. 8.30% in 2026E.  
Reiterate Buy; trim PO to NT$2,310 
We raise our 2026-28E EPS by 4-14%, mainly to reflect the 3Q26 beat and solid sales 
growth aided by general and ASIC server projects. We however trim PO to NT$2,310 
(15x 4Q26-3Q27E EPS) from NT$2,387 (18x 2H26-1H27E EPS), as we roll over our 
valuation base and lower multiple on macro uncertainties and concerns over margin 
decline. Reit. Buy on robust ASIC momentum and a constructive general server outlook.

Company Sector 
IT Hardware

Company Description 
Wiwynn supplies server, storage and cloud equipment to 
global cloud service providers (CSPs). 100% of its sales 
comes from server/datacenter business. Key clients are 
Meta, Microsoft, and Amazon. Wiwynn was founded in April 
2012 and was listed on Taiwan Stock Exchange on 27 
March 2019. As of 2Q26, Wistron (3231 TT) owned 35.27% 
of shares in Wiwynn. Besides the headquarter in Taiwan, its 
subsidiaries and operations are located in Malaysia, Hong 
Kong, China, South Korea, Japan, the US and Mexico.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Wiwynn as we are positive on 
increasing contribution from AI server projects, delivery in 
Amazon after efforts for years, and regular server 
bottoming out from 2H23, driving double-digit 
sales/earnings growth in 2026-28E.

### Page 5

Price objective basis & risk 
Wiwynn (XWIQF) 
Our PO of NT$2,310 is based on 15x 4Q26-3Q27E EPS, which is 1.5-2.0SD above 
Wiwynn's historical P/E average since listing. We believe such a multiple is reasonable to 
reflect Wiwynn's rising sales contribution from AI server, delivery in Amazon projects, 
and regular server demand bottoming out from 2H23.

Upside risks to our PO are: (1) higher-than-expected capex growth from key clients, (2) 
stronger-than-expected momentum in server/datacenter infrastructure, (3) more project 
wins/launches for AI server, and (4) much higher contribution from the third largest 
customer.

Downside risks to our PO are: (1) unexpected slowdown of key clients' capex spending in 
server/datacenter, (2) more intensified competition from peers, and (3) weaker 
momentum in AI server projects.

of potential price fluctuation, are: A - Low, B - Medium and C - High. INVESTMENT RATINGS reflect the analyst’s assessment of both a stock’s absolute total return 
potential as well as its attractiveness for investment relative to other stocks within its Coverage Cluster (defined below). Our investment ratings are: 1 -  Buy stocks 
are expected to have a total return of at least 10% and are the most attractive stocks in the coverage cluster; 2 - Neutral stocks are expected to remain flat or 
increase in value and are less attractive than Buy rated stocks and 3 - Underperform stocks are the least attractive stocks in a coverage cluster. An investment 
rating of 6 (No Rating) indicates that a stock is no longer trading on the basis of fundamentals. Analysts assign investment ratings considering, among other things, 
the 0-12 month total return expectation for a stock and the firm’s guidelines for ratings dispersions (shown in the table below). The current price objective for a 
stock should be referenced to better understand the total return expectation at any given time. The price objective reflects the analyst’s view of the potential price

### Page 1

Advanced Energy Solution (6781.TW)
Growth Intact Despite A Longer HVDC Transition
CITI'S TAKE 
We walked away from AES’s 3Q briefing more confident in the BBU growth 
outlook. Resilient low-voltage demand and higher-power product 
upgrades should sustain growth through the HVDC transition, with 
400V /800V commercialization targeted for mid/end-2027E respectively. 
Rising technical complexity should favor AES’s engineering expertise and 
established customer relationships. We trim our TP to NT$1,550 (from 
NT$1,680) on 25x 2027E P/E from 27x reflecting HVDC timing uncertainty. 
Reiterate Buy as rising BBU penetration and content per rack underpin 
structural growth.
3Q26: strong BBU demand drives growth and mix upgrades lift GPM y/y — 3Q 
sales of NT$5.2bn rose 27% y/y and 9% q/q, driven by strong BBU demand and were 
9% above our forecast. GPM expanded 1.3ppt y/y to 36.3% on mix upgrades, though 
it fell 2.8ppt q/q. Op profits rose 34% y/y with OPM up 1.4ppt y/y to 24.6%. Net profit 
of NT$1.1bn was up 34% y/y, 3% above our forecast but 5% below consensus. Higher 
opex and lower non-op income partly offset the sales beat.
Low voltage BBUs retain a substantial growth runway — Mgmt expects steady 4Q 
growth and remains positive on 2027E, with no downward revisions to customer 
demand plans. Continued datacenter buildout should sustain low-voltage BBU 
procurement while HVDC architectures undergo qualifications. Demand for 3kW 
products remains broad across conventional servers and lower power datacenter 
workloads. AES is working with several new CSPs and overseas SIs, with new 
customer contributions expected in 2027E. Rising GPU power, greater BBU 
penetration and higher content per MW should support volumes and mix-led ASP 
growth. Cell prices are rising, but AES indicates stable procurement through long-
standing relationships with suppliers and will seek to pass on cost increases. 
HVDC progress: commercialization likely in 2H27E — High voltage BBU 
commercialization has been delayed to 2H27E, reflecting product development and 
system integration challenges. Currently samples have been delivered and large-
scale testing is underway for 400V products, with mass production targeted for 
mid-2027E and 800V products are scheduled for end-2027E. Mgmt. sees high 
voltage BBU ASP to be 5-10x higher than low voltage products. We believe 
increasing system complexity and qualification requirements favor AES’s 
established engineering capabilities and CSP relationships, though shipment 
timing and margin contribution require monitoring.

Non-BBU development: new applications broaden the growth base — Mgmt
expects non-BBU growth to outpace BBU growth in 2027E as new projects scale.
LEV demand should gradually recover following the inventory correction. Telecom
application has entered mass production in 2026, with mgmt. expecting the
business to double in 2027E. AGV, AMR, forklift and robotics projects involve
leading international customers, with AGV /AMR products expected to enter mass

production in 2H27E. These projects should diversify revenue and extend AES’s
battery pack expertise into additional markets.
Earnings revisions and TP change —We lift our earnings forecasts by 6% for
2026E mainly to reflect stronger sales strength and GPM in 2026E while we keep
our 2027 /2028E earnings estimates largely unchanged. We slightly lower our TP to
NT$1,550 based on 25x 2027E P/E vs. 27x prior reflecting HVDC timing uncertainty.

### Page 5

Advanced Energy Solution (6781.TW)
09 November 2026 Citi Research
5
Advanced Energy Solution
Company description
Established in January 2020 and listed in March 2021, Advanced Energy
Solution Holdings (AES) is a holding company registered in Cayman Island,
supplying battery packs for LEV (light electrical vehicle such as e-Bikes) and
BBU (battery back-up unit for datacenters) applications. Prior to the
establishment and IPO, AES was a BU focusing on non-IT applications within

its parent company, Simplo Technology (6121.TW).
Investment strategy
We rate AES shares as Buy. Being an industry leader in battery pack design
and manufacturing with a proven track record, we expect AES to benefit from
higher BBU content per rack and deep CSP engagement as the industry
transitions from conventional 48V systems to HVDC architecture. BBU now
contributes > 70% of sales and should remain the key earnings driver as AI
rack power continues to rise. We forecast earnings CAGR of 27% in 2025-

2028E, supported by strong BBU demand, mix upgrade toward high-power
BBU products and op leverage.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$1,550 is based on 25x 2027E EPS on par with its
historical average since listing and supported by a 2025-28E earnings CAGR
of 27%. We use 2027E as the year of valuation base as we think 2027E can
better reflect its earnings power after the commercializarion of HVDC
products.
Risks
Key downside risks to our earnings forecasts that could prevent the shares

from reaching our target price include: 1) slower-than-expected end demand
from e-Bikes post the pandemic and volaitlity in cloud capex deployment that
could impact datacenter BBU sales; 2) component supply
constraints/logistics challenges, which could lead to slower-than-expected
shipment and top-line momentum; and 3) peer competition, which could
impact sales growth and margins negatively, in our view.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

Micro-Star (2377.TW)
3Q26 Preview: Margins to Stay Elevated on Cost Pass-Through; Raise 
TP to NT$140, Stay Neutral
CITI'S TAKE 
MSI will report 3Q26 results in mid-Nov. We now expect net profit of 
NT$3.19bn and EPS of NT$3.77, 18% above con estimates, as 3Q26 GPM 
stays elevated at 15.8% vs. our previous 12.0% estimates, supported by 
better VGA supply and continued ASP pass-through of rising memory 
costs. For 4Q26E, we expect MB/VGA shipments to ease QoQ but ASPs to 
rise, with server contribution to further increase. Looking into 2027E, we 
expect PC demand to remain weak and pass-through room to narrow as 
memory price increases pace slows down. We raise 2026E-28E earnings by 
10-57% and lift our TP to NT$140 (from NT$118; 12x 2027E EPS). We stay 
Neutral, with likely limited upside given uncertain PC recovery and modest 
server contribution. 
3Q26 preview — MSI reported 3Q26 revenue of NT$56.9bn (+14% QoQ/-1% YoY), 
broadly in line with Citi’s forecast and slightly ahead of cons. While Sep MB and VGA 
shipments both exceeded expectations, as VGA supply improved. With memory and 
other raw material prices continuing to rise and MSI able to pass costs through, we 
expect 3Q26 GPM to stay elevated at 15.8%, well above our previous 12.0% estimate. 
Assuming only a modest increase in opex ratio, we forecast net profit of NT$3.19bn 
(+6% QoQ/+88% YoY) and EPS of NT$3.77, 18% above cons forecasts.
Price hike continues — We expect 4Q26E shipments to decline from 3Q26’s peak 
season. Mgmt. also indicated that Oct shipment could go down from Sep high base. 
However, we raise our 4Q26E pricing expectations and believe ASP should stay 
elevated, as memory prices keep climbing and MSI continues to pass costs through. 
The long-cultivated server business should also contribute higher than previous 
quarters. Looking into 2027, PC demand should remain weak as component costs 
stay high; we expect MSI’s MB/VGA/PC shipments to decline by double digits, with 
pass-through room narrowing as the price hike pace slows down.
Implications — We raise our 2026E-28E earnings forecasts by 10-57%, reflecting 
sustained strong component cost pass-through. We lift our TP to NT$140 (from 
NT$118), implying 12x 2027E EPS (rolled over from 12x 2026E EPS). We believe the 
share price already reflects MSI’s ability to pass costs through to end products, and 
we see limited upside, given that the PC demand recovery remains highly uncertain 
and the contribution from the new server business is still limited. Stay Neutral.

Bull/Bear: Micro-Star (2377.TW)

### Page 6

Micro-Star (2377.TW)
09 November 2026 Citi Research
6
Micro-Star
Company description
Established in 1986 and listed in 1998, Taiwan-based MSI is a leading
OBM/ODM for gaming PCs, motherboards, graphic cards, industrial PCs, and
servers. MSI started business as a motherboard manufacturer but
transformed itself to become a leading gaming PC play in 2012 with a
comprehensive product portfolio across the gaming system and components.
MSI enjoys about 16% global share in the gaming NB industry, and is ranked

No.3 in graphic card shipments and No.3 in motherboard shipments globally.
Investment strategy
We rate MSI shares at Neutral as we anticipate earnings upward pressure
from the delince in PC and MB/VGA shipments in the near-term. While the
soaring component costs could be partially offset by price hike, we expect
muted demand and AI resource crowding to wiegh on margins upside. Further
upside maintains limited, though, until we see a meaningful recovery in core

gaming demand or a more substantial contribution from the server business.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$140 is based on 12x 2027E EPS, below the stock's five-
year average forward 12-month PE. This valuation reflects the earnings
downside risks from muted PC demand and margin pressure from soaring
component costs, while accounting for the time required for the server
business to achieve meaningful scale.
Risks
Key upside risks that could cause MSI's shares to trade above our target price

include: 1) a sharper-than-expected recovery in high-end gaming demand;
and 2) faster-than-anticipated revenue contribution from the AI server
segment. Key downside risks include: 1) a deeper-than-expected contraction
in global PC shipments; 2) prolonged supply shortages for gaming GPUs due
to Ai resource crowding; and 3) inability to pass through surging memory and
CPU costs to price-sensitive consumers.
If you are visually impaired and would like to speak to a Citi representative regarding the details

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: We review GCE’s 3Q26 results, released on 10 November.

What’s the impact: 3Q26 beat Bloomberg consensus. GCE’s 3Q26 
earnings came in at TWD6.39bn (EPS: TWD12.4), in line with our estimate 
and 3% higher vs. the street’s. While revenue was 7% lower than our 
expectation due to flooding in Thailand and product transition, gross margin 
expanded by 4pp QoQ to 38.7% mainly due to easing raw-material cost 
inflation and its own price increases. However, net income was impacted by 
higher non-op loss of TWD238m (likely due to FX loss) though offset by a 
lower effective tax rate of 25% as Thailand plant turned more profitable.

Business outlook. Looking ahead, GCE now guides for revenue to be 
flattish QoQ in 4Q26. We suspect that AMZN’s ASIC AI server gradually 
shifting its model from air-cooled (2 chipsets per mainboard) to liquid-
cooled (4 chipsets) and the year-end inventory-taking are the key reasons. 
However, we expect the stagnant revenue momentum to be short-lived and 
growth momentum to resume with new projects (including Google’s TPU 
and MSFT’s Maia) contributing from 1H27.

In 2H27, we expect new capacity ramps at its 2nd plant in Thailand and 2nd 
plant in Suzhou (acquisition of Pegatron’s land; across the 1st plant in 
Suzhou) to be growth drivers, which could further increase its allocation in 
major ASIC AI server projects. As for concerns over the thicker-HDI board 
(a combination of sequential lamination HLC and HDI together) that we 
highlighted in in our previous note (2Q26 results review: profitability missed 
slightly), we believe GCE has already overcome the difficulties for 3-
lamination HDI and can now successfully produce AMZN’s requirement on 
its Trn4 AI server heavy HDI board. However, GCE might still need to 
achieve the requirement for Google’s TPU v9 which requires c.5-6 
lamination techniques. We believe the capacity expansion in 2027 would 
not be enough to catch up with AI demand growth in 2027. As such, we 
believe it will further drive price hikes in CCL raw-material, CCL, and PCB, 
and see gross margin (GM) expansion over our forecast horizon. We raise 
our GM forecasts to 40.5%/41.3% (prior: 38.0/40.2%) for 2027-28E.

What we recommend: We raise our 2027-28E EPS by 1-10% to reflect its 
3Q26 results and higher GM on current supply-demand dynamics. We 
reaffirm our Buy (1) call but trim our 12M TP to TWD1,600 with a lower 
PER target of 21x (prior: 26x; vs. past-3-year range of 7-28x), on our 4-
quarter-forward diluted EPS, to reflect weaker market sentiment and on-
going concerns over HDI. Key risk: worse-than-expected AI server demand.

How we differ: Our 2027-28E earnings are 4-5% higher than consensus, 
likely due to our higher profitability assumptions.

Company profile 
Founded in 1981 and listed on the Taiwan Stock Exchange in 1998, Gold Circuit Electronics (GCE) was, 
in its early years, one of the world’s top-3 printed circuit board (PCB) vendors providing double-sided 
and multi-layer PCBs for notebooks. However, as the notebook market became saturated from 2011, 
GCE started reshuffling its revenue mix to more high-end products (servers, workstations and 
networking boards). The requirement of higher-layer PCBs from these products has not only proved 
GCE’s manufacturing capabilities but also created a high entry barrier. GCE’s revenue contribution from 
the server and networking segments has surged to over 80% in 2024 (from 37% in 2010), the highest 
among its PCB peers.

### Page 5

5

Gold Circuit Electronics (2368 TT): 10 November 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 10 Nov 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
GCE's Board consists of 9 directors, including 3 independent directors. The number of
independent directors is above Taiwan securities law's requirement of more than two and
exceeds one-fifth of the total number of directors on the board. The backgrounds of the
independent directors are diverse with expertise in the areas of business management, finance
& audit, and law, which should be positive for executive and board quality.
That said, the roles of CEO and chairman are held by one person, who is a second-generation
family member of GCE’s founder. The organisation structure appears to us to be a family
business, which could lead to conflicts of interest between key executives and outside
shareholders.
Capital management 2
GCE declared a DPS of TWD10.0 in 2025, another record high. GCE has declared cash
dividends for six consecutive years from 2020 to 2025, following a period of intermittent payouts
mainly due to fierce competition in the PCB industry (especially in notebook applications) and
high earnings volatility from its 3 plants in China caused by historically low utilisation rates. We
believe the company’s strategy to focus on high-end products (eg, servers and networking) has
started bearing fruit, which was also the main reason GCE was able to declare a record-high
cash dividend in 2025. Overall, we expect GCE to continue to benefit from ASP and gross-
margin expansion, driven by server and networking spec upgrades, which should be value-
accretive for investors in the long term.
Related party &
transaction 1 Sales to related parties were 0% of total 2025 sales and purchases from related parties were also 0% of total 2025 purchases. We see limited risk from related-party transactions.
S Supply chain management 1
GCE is a multi-layer PCB manufacturer with major raw materials, such as thin core, copper foil,
pre-preg, copper ball, dry film, solder mask and various chemical materials used in the
manufacturing process. GCE has acquired the ISO 9001 and IECQ QC 080000 certifications to
improve its supply chain management capabilities in product quality and hazardous material
usage. Like most hardware vendors, GCE is also committed to refusing procurement of conflict
minerals and complying with the Responsible Business Alliance (RBA) Code of Conduct.
According to its 2025 annual report, over 99% of its top 40 suppliers returned their RBA
commitment letters. As part of its RBA compliance efforts, GCE holds an annual supply chain
meeting to review the achievement rate of its previous targets and set new goals for suppliers. In
2025, GCE audited 154 suppliers, including 114 on-site audits and 40 document reviews,
achieving a 100% completion rate against its audit targets. The company also evaluates new
suppliers based on ISO 9001, ISO 14001 and IATF 16949 certification requirements and
conducts annual supplier assessments.
S Data security 2
Information security is one of the key topics under the company’s risk management guidelines.
GCE constructed an information security system, under the “information security committee”, to
minimise the risk of information loss, sabotage, and leakage caused by human errors, malicious
destructions or natural disasters. Below are several measures the company has taken to
improve data security: GCE will conduct regular awareness training to ensure employees'
security awareness and fulfilment of information security system requirements. Also, the
information security committee will develop an information security continuity plan and conduct
drills to ensure continuous operation of the company.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

研 究  報 告                
十銓(4967.TT) 
產業：電子–半導體 
深化 B2B 布局開啟成⻑動能 
 投資評價與建議 
2026年營運受惠記憶體價格上漲及產品組合優化，2027年隨著新廠投產、企
業級客戶導入及邊緣AI需求；若公司能維持穩定貨源並有效控制庫存成本，獲
利有望維持高檔。 
 重點評論分析 
受惠記憶體價格漲勢持續 ， 推升營收獲利 ：3Q26營收季增33.6%至101.4億元 ，
年增143.5%，受惠記憶體報價上漲、產品組合優化帶動毛利率上升至49.3%，
營業利益率44.5%，本期淨利36.18億元，稅後EPS 37.19元，優於預期。 
庫存調配、產品售價同步拉升，有望支撐獲利：終端市況需求呈現分化，其中，  
AI資料中心跟企業伺服器、邊緣運算等較高階需求強，約佔整體DRAM產出
70% ；另消費端換機需求受到零組件成本上漲壓抑整體需求， 惟高階電競超
頻與AI創作者在快速運算跟大容量需求下仍強。在原廠優先生產資源向HBM、
高階DDR5傾斜下，除了減少DDR4亦排擠其他應用市場產能，將使工控、網
通、⾞用等⻑週期性客戶，⾯臨缺料與成本上升壓⼒。尤其部分工控及網通應
用仍依賴DDR4。截3Q26季底庫存達172億元，其中，DRAM約佔75%、Flash
約25%，公司現⾏庫存策略係以伺服器、工控⻑單及高階B2C為主。時序進入
4Q26，公司雖並未給予財測，但基於庫存調配及產品結構持續優化下，推估
4Q26營收107.９億元，毛利率52.3%，稅後EPS 48.6元。 
 投資風險 
記憶體價格反轉與庫存跌價風險、終端需求疲軟

### Page 2

2 
| 營運概況 
受惠記憶體價格漲勢持續，推升營收獲利：3Q26 營收季增 33.6%至 101.4 億元，年增 143.5%，受惠記憶體報價
上漲、產品組合優化帶動毛利率上升至 49.3%，營業利益率 44.5%，本期淨利 36.18 億元，稅後 EPS 37.19 元，
優於預期。市場別部份，至前三季 B2B(企業暨工控應用)營收從去年同期的 24.6 上升至 64.6%，B2C(品牌暨通路)
則降至 35.40%。 
庫存調配、產品售價同步拉升，有望支撐獲利：終端市況需求呈現分化，其中，AI 資料中心跟企業伺服器、邊緣
運算等較高階需求強，約佔整體 DRAM 產出 70%；另消費端換機需求受到零組件成本上漲壓抑整體需求，惟高
階電競超頻與 AI 創作者在快速運算跟大容量需求下仍強。在原廠優先生產資源向 HBM、高階 DDR5 傾斜下，除
了減少 DDR4 亦排擠其他應用市場產能，將使工控、網通、⾞用等⻑週期性客戶，⾯臨缺料與成本上升壓⼒。尤
其部分工控及網通應用仍依賴 DDR4。截 3Q26 季底庫存達 172 億元，其中，DRAM 約佔 75%、Flash 約 25%，
在庫存量部份，DDR4 占比約 60.09%，DDR5 約 38.51%，主要較早布局 DDR4 庫存；公司現⾏庫存策略係以伺
服器 、 工控⻑單及高階 B2C 為主 。 進入 4Q26 ， 公司並未給予財測 ， 但基於庫存調配及產品結構優化下 ， 推估 4Q26
營收 107.9 億元，毛利率 52.3%，稅後 EPS 48.6 元。 
2027 年前無價格下行趨勢：展望 2027 年，管理階層表示除了三大原廠之外，包含中國系廠記憶體廠，目前產量
以及未來 1~3 年陸續新增的產量，預期整體供需平衡至少要至 2028 年下半年或以後，且 4Q26 至 2Q27 係為缺
貨最嚴峻時期，2027 年價格難以見到反轉。產能規劃方⾯，新北約千坪自動化新廠預計 1Q27 進⾏裝修及設備進
駐 ， 2Q27 完工投產 ， 整體產能預估增加約 50% 。 十銓將事業群分為企業暨工控應用(B2B) ， 以及品牌暨通路(B2C) ， 
B2B 以伺服器 、 IPC 工控及邊緣 AI 為主要拓展 ， 提供記憶體與儲存方案整合 、 客製韌體 、 技術支援及可靠度驗證 。
隨著 AI 推論由大型資料中心向邊緣端擴散，不同終端設備對溫度、功耗、可靠度需求多元，具備技術支援及客製
化能⼒的模組廠，有助提高客戶黏著度與產品附加價值。另在 B2C 業務方⾯，公司持續深耕 T-FORCE 電競及 T-
CREATE 創作者市場，同時，過去十年在消費性產品中，高階電競與創作者模組占比已超過七成；在消費者對高效
能、大容量及超頻產品仍有需求的情況下，有助降低低階 PC 需求疲弱衝擊。整體而言，2026 年營運受惠記憶體
價格上漲及 B2B 比重提高，2027 年隨著新廠投產、企業級客戶導入及邊緣 AI 需求；若能維持穩定貨源並有效控
制庫存成本，獲利有望維持高檔。預估 2027 年營收 436.8 億元，毛利率 46.1%，稅後 EPS 169.64 元，目標價
340 元(2 倍 PER*2027EPS(F))。

緯穎 (6669  TT)  Trading Buy

雖預期 伺 服 器 出 貨 量持續 成長 ， 惟 現 階 段 AI 前 景 疑 慮 變
大， 投資建議 Trading Buy 。  
投 資 建 議 ： 基於： (1)Trainium 3 平 台 持 續 放 量 ， 接 續 的 液 冷 版
本 ASP 將 顯 著 高 於 氣 冷 版 本 ，(2) 預期 4Q26 Vera Rubin 和 Helios
小 量 出 貨，並 於 1H27 放 量，GPU 系 列 將 成 ASIC 外 另 一 成 長 引
擎。(3) 通 用 型 伺 服 器 需 求 強 勁，可 望 延 續 至 2027 。然 而 FY3Q26 
CSP 巨 頭 財 報 公 布 後， 又 逢 美 國 期 中 選 舉 反 資 料 中 心 建 置 的 民
主黨大勝 ， 市 場 對 於 AI 投 資 擴 張 疑 慮 上 升 ， 故 群 益 給 予 緯 穎
Trading Buy 的 投 資 建 議 ， 目 標 價 1,800 元。  
通 用 型 與 ASIC 提 供 營 運 支 撐，預 計 Trainium 3 液冷將於 2027
年 放 量： 根 據 我 們 調 查 ， 預 估2026 年 Trainium 3 液 冷 版 本 出 貨
量 將 略 低 預 期，並 可 如 期 於 1H27 放量 。目 前 緯 穎 的 成 長 基 礎 仍
在 通 用 型 及 ASIC 伺 服 器 ，GPU 新 平 台 則 成 為 未 來 另 一 重 要 成
長引擎。  
Vera Rubin 與  Helios 擴 大 產 品 布 局： 緯 穎 於 10/2026 OCP 中
表 示 其 與 緯 創 為 NVIDIA Vera Rubin NVL72 首批 Pilot Build 
Partners 之 一，將 參 與 驗 證 及 design -in，同 場 展 示 的 AMD Helios
則採用 MI450 系列 GPU 機 櫃 ， 預 期 4Q26 後段 GPU 新 平 台 可
小 量 出 貨 ， 並 於 1H27 放量。  
AI 投 資 疑 慮 上 升： 隨 著 FY3Q26 各 大 主 要 雲 端 廠(CSP) 財 報 公
布 ， 市 場 對 於 其 資 本 支 出 是 否 可 維 持 高 檔 存 在 疑 慮 。 此 外 ， 隨
著 11/2026 美 國 期 中 選 舉 結 束 ， 投 資 人 擔 憂 資 料 中 心 建 置 進 度
亦 可 能 放 緩 ， 後 續 可 能 對 緯 穎 營 運 成 長 動 能 造 成 影 響 。

25,968.39 億元， YoY+69.45% ； AI 伺 服 器 較 高 價 的 零 組 件 將 造
成 毛 利 率 稀 釋 ， 毛 利 率 7.74% ， YoY -0.9ppts ，營益率 5.69% ，

個股報告

2 群益投顧 
  ESG  
環 境：緯 穎 宣 示「 2030 年 達 成 全 球 100% 再 生 能 源 使 用、2040 年 邁 向 淨 零 排
放 」， 減 碳 目 標 已 通 過 SBTi 審 核 。2025 年 全 球 再 生 能 源 使 用 率 達 80.90% ，
廢 棄 物 回 收 利 用 率 達 82.00% ， 並 透 過 冰 水 主 機 溫 度 優 化 、 生 產 設 備 節 能 及
LED 照 明 汰 換 等 措 施 ， 全 年 節 電 630,678 度 ， 相 當 於 減 少 298.74 公噸 CO₂e
排 放。此 外，公 司 推 動 木 箱 及 棧 板 循 環 使 用，2025 年 減 少 約 2,565 公 噸 廢 棄
物 ， 創 造 約 6.7 億 元 經 濟 效 益 ， 並 逐 步 導 入 內 部 碳 定 價 機 制 ， 將 減 碳 效 益 納
入 投 資 決 策 。 
社 會 ： 緯 穎 重 視 人 才 培 育 、 員 工 福 利 及 社 會 參 與 ， 持 續 推 動 全 球 線 上 學 習 、
專 業 職 能 訓 練 及 多 元 共 融 職 場 。2025 年 全 球 員 工 平 均 受 訓 時 數 達 34 小 時 ，
間 接 人 員 平 均 約 50 小 時 ， 並 透 過 職 涯 發 展 及 員 工 溝 通 機 制 強 化 人 才 留 任 。
對 外 則 積 極 投 入 產 學 合 作 、 實 習 人 才 培 育 、 社 區 關 懷 及 環 境 保 育 ，2025 年
社 會 投 入 金 額 達 2,819 萬 元，並 透 過 緯 穎 永 續 基 金 會 推 動 公 益 活 動，累 計 投
入 全 球 植 樹 57,782 棵 及 珊 瑚 復 育 8,296 株 ， 持 續 擴 大 企 業 社 會 影 響 力 。 
治 理 ： 緯 穎 以 董 事 會 為 公 司 治 理 最 高 機 構 ， 設 有 審 計 委 員 會 、 薪 資 報 酬 委
員 會、提 名 委 員 會 及 企 業 永 續 發 展 委 員 會，定 期 檢 視 公 司 治 理 與 永 續 發 展 績
效。公司將 ESG 績 效 納 入 高 階 經 理 人 薪 酬 考 核 ， 並 建 立 風 險 暨 安 全 管 理 機
制，每年至少兩次向審計委員會及董事會報告。此外，依循責任商業聯盟
（ RBA ） 準 則 落 實 誠 信 經 營 與 反 貪 腐 管 理 ，2025 年 全 球 員 工 行 為 準 則 簽 署
率 及 教 育 訓 練 涵 蓋 率 均 達 100% ， 當 年 度 未 發 生 違 反 誠 信 道 德 或 涉 及 貪 腐 之
情事。  
公 司 簡 介 
緯 穎 科 技（6669 TT ）成 立 於 2012 年 ， 為 緯 創 集 團 旗 下 專 注 於 超 大 規 模 資 料
中 心 （Hyperscale Data Center ） 及 雲 端 基 礎 架 構 的 伺 服 器 設 計 與 製 造 商，2019
年 於 台 灣 證 券 交 易 所 掛 牌 上 市。公 司 主 要 提 供 雲 端 運 算、人 工 智 慧 及 資 料 中
心 所 需 之 伺 服 器、儲 存 設 備、網 路 系 統 及 機 櫃 基 礎 設 施，並 提 供 客 製 化 產 品
設 計、系 統 整 合 及 軟 硬 體 解 決 方 案。緯 穎 為 開 放 運 算 計 畫 （OCP ） 白 金 會 員，
主要採 ODM Direct 商 業 模 式 ， 直 接 服 務 全 球 大 型 雲 端 服 務 供 應 商（CSP ），
產 品 涵 蓋 通 用 型 伺 服 器 、AI GPU 伺 服 器 及 客 製 化 ASIC 伺 服 器 ， 並 提 供 L6
主 機 板、L10 整 機 及 L11 整 櫃 等 不 同 層 級 的 系 統 整 合 服 務。公 司 以 台 灣 為 研
發 及 營 運 核 心，於 墨 西 哥、馬 來 西 亞 及 美 國 等 地 布 局 生 產 與 服 務 據 點，主 要
台 灣 同 業 競 爭 對 手 包 括 鴻 海（2317 TT ）、 廣 達（ 2382 TT ）。 2023 年 地 區 別
營 收 比 重 為 ： 美 洲 78.58% 、歐洲 11.90% 、 亞 洲 7.73% 、 其 他 地 區 1.21% 、
台灣內銷 0.57% ， 顯 示 公 司 營 收 高 度 集 中 於 美 洲 市 場 。

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 11 月 6 日

6 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15 %

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係「 首 次 」給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時，其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個月
目 標 價 之 潛 在 上 漲 空 間 計 算 而 得 。 個 股 投 資 評 等 分 為四 個 等 級 ， 定 義 如 上 。
爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及 「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一 旦 我 們 給 予 特 定 個 股「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」或「 區 間 操 作 」之 投 資 評 等，
就 是 責 任 的 開 始，爾 後 將 透 過 各 式 研 究 報 告 作 定 期 性、持 續 性 追 蹤 基 本 面 及
股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ， 研
判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 。

【免責聲明】  
  本 研 究 報 告 僅 提 供 予 特 定 人 之 客 戶 作 為 參 考 資 料「 非 經 同 意 不 得 轉 載 」 。我
們 並 不 確 保 此 資 訊 的 完 整 性 與 正 確 性，投 資 人 應 了 解，報 告 中 有 關 未 來 預 測
之 陳 述 可 能 不 會 實 現，因 而 不 應 被 依 賴。而 且 此 報 告 並 非 根 據 特 定 投 資 目 的
或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的，既 非 對
投 資 人 於 買 賣 證 券、選 擇 權、期 貨 或 其 他 證 券 相 關 之 衍 生 性 商 品 提 供 詢 價 服
務，亦 非 作 為 進 行 交 易 的 要 約。投 資 人 應 注 意 到 相 關 證 券 之 價 值 及 收 益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

자료: 유안타증권

Company Report  2026.11.12

상선, 방산, 해양, 엔진 모든 사업 영위하는 전천후 기업 
경쟁사 대비 1) 가장 넓은 사업 영역을 보유, 2) 시가총액 또한 높음. 이러한 특성은 동사의 
사업 포트폴리오 균형 관점에서 장점이나, 반대로 투자 매력의 집중을 일정 부분 희석. 경쟁
사 대비 주가 상승 트리거가 가장 폭넓게 존재하나, 상승 탄력은 그만큼 제한. 
상선: FY27F 매출인식은 FY23-25 수주 물량, 가스선 매출비중 하락 
FY27F 상선부문 매출은 FY23-25 수주 물량이 인식되며 (일부 FY26F 수주), FY25 수주
한 물량이 연간 40% 수준의 가장 높은 매출기여도를 보일 것. 2H27F부터 올해 수주한 일
부 선박의 매출인식 또한 발생하며, 연중 가스선 매출 비중이 하락하고 컨테이너선 매출 비
중이 상승. 
방산: 좋게 보자면 불안하고, 나쁘게 보자면 아쉬운 
MASGA를 국내 야드에서 진행한다는 대전제로 경쟁사와 같은 적정가치를 부여해 왔으나, 
미국 현지 건조와 같은 여러 가능성을 고려하면 다음과 같은 불확실성 존재: 1) 중간지주사
와의 미국내 JV 설립 관련 투자 지분 미정, 2) 신규 조선소 건립시 미국내 수주와 매출까지 
걸리는 시간. 
투자의견 BUY 유지, 다 깎아서 봐도 충분한 상승여력 
상선부문 26.5조, 방산 8조, 선박엔진 9조, 발전엔진 (FY30F 기준) 13.5조 수준의 SOTP 
밸류에이션 기반하여 목표주가 643,000원 제시. 발전엔진 증설로 인한 미래가치를 반영하
나, 상선부문 Target P/O multiple 하향 (0.7x, 업종 공통)과 MASGA 적정가치 하향으로 
목표주가 자체는 하락. 보수적 멀티플 제시함에도 주가 상승 여력은 충분히 확보된 상황. 업
종 전체적 긍정요인 외의 동사 주가 상승 트리거는 1) 미국 현지 조선소 투자주체 발탁, 2) 
DC향 발전엔진 추가 수주, 3) MASGA 국내 건조 물량 수주.

Document 354: European resilience, American vigour (2026-10-30)

Source PDF: all report pdf/European resilience, American vigour (2026-10-30).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 30 October 2026

EcoFlash

European resilience, American vigour

Eurozone growth continues to prove resilient, notwithstanding the volatility of the Irish figures. The Q3 data reinforce 
our forecast of 1.1% growth this year, which could even be slightly exceeded, as German and Spanish momentum shows 
no sign of fading. In France, the negative surprise (-0.1% q/q against a consensus of +0.1% q/q) stems from a new one-
off factor (the sharp fall in aerospace output caused by delays in input deliveries) and masks an encouraging rebound 
in household consumption. In the United  States, growth reached its highest level since Q2 2025 (0.8% q/q), driven by 
buoyant private domestic demand.

Eurozone: The resilience of growth is confirmed 
Eurozone GDP grew by 0.4% q/q in Q3  according to Eurostat's preliminary flash estimate, above both the consensus and our 
Nowcast (0.3%). The ever -volatile Irish figures contributed 0.2 pp to this outcome. Excluding Ireland, growth (0.2% q/q) 
nonetheless remains in line with the pace of recent quarters (0.2 -0.3% q/q on average). This illustrates the Eurozone's capacity 
to withstand the current shocks, thanks in particular to the strength of Spain (+0.07 pp contribution to the q/q change in Eurozone’s 
GDP) and the Netherlands (+0.04 pp), and to Germany's gradual recovery (+0.06 pp). France (-0.03 pp) and Italy (zero growth), 
by contrast, posted disappointing results, although in France's case these partly reflect temporary factors (aerospace output). The 
growth carry-over for 2026 now stands at 1.0%, which supports our forecast of 1.1% in annual average terms and even points to 
upside risk. The European Commission's October surveys remain upbeat, with a further improvement in industrial activity 
expectations. 
The German recovery continues . As anticipated by our  Nowcast, real GDP grew by 0.2% q/q in Q3 (after 0.3% in Q2).  
According to Destatis, consumption (private and public) increased slightly, while investment edged down. Export momentum 
slowed markedly compared with Q2. This is Germany's fourth consecutive quarter of growth. 
Italian GDP surprised on the downside, stagnating in Q3 (against a consensus of 0.1%).  According to Istat, this reflects a 
decline in value added in industry, offset by increases in services and agriculture. The positive contribution from domestic demand 
was reportedly cancelled out by a negative contribution from net trade. 
Spanish growth remained robust in Q3, in line with expectations (0.6% q/q, after 0.7% in Q2),  driven mainly by domestic 
demand (0.5 pp contribution) and, to a lesser extent, by net exports (0.1 pp). Consumption remains the main engine of growth 
(+0.6% q/q for private consumption and +0.9% for public consumption). Investment rose by 0.7%. Export gr owth (1.3%) once 
again outpaced import growth (1.1%) this quarter. All sectors except construction recorded positive changes in value added, with 
industry and services both up 0.7%. 
France: A sharp drop in aerospace output prevents a return to growth in Q3 
GDP contracted by 0.1% q/q in Q3 (after stagnating in Q2), weighed down by a sharp fall in aerospace output (-4.5% q/q 
in the production of other transport equipment, which includes aerospace). This steep decline dragged down goods production 
(down 1.2% q/q) and explains the sharp contraction in exports ( -1.7% q/q) and business investment (-0.4% q/q). Net trade thus 
subtracted 0.6 pp from GDP (imports were flat over the same period). Conversely, household consumption surprised on the 
upside (+0.4% q/q), boosted by purchases of electric vehicles (registrations up 112% y/y in Q3). Public consumption 
(+0.3% q/q) also supported growth, as did activity in market services (+0.3% q/q).  Despite the sharp drop in aerospace 
output in Q3, Airbus has confirmed its delivery targets for the year (with engine supply problems expected to be resolved), pointing 
to a strong rebound in growth in Q4 (0.3% q/q according to our forecasts). Overall, gr owth is expected to reach 0.3% in 2026 , 
before picking up to 0.8% in 2027. 
United States: Growth at a one-year high  
GDP growth picked up in Q3 to 3.4% at a seasonally adjusted annualised rate ( AR; i.e. 0.8% q/q), from 2.2% in Q2 
(0.6% q/q). This is the fastest pace since Q3 2025, confirming that activity is almost immune to the energy shock. Private domestic 
demand remains particularly strong. Private non-residential investment accelerated to 12.7% AR, a performance unmatched since 
the post-Covid reopening. AI-related components are driving this figure (18.5% AR), but other types of investment are also rising 
for a second consecutive quarter (3.8% AR) after contracting uninterruptedly throughout 2025. Household consumption, steady 
at 2.5% AR in real terms, is growing faster than it did before inflation picked up again, but at the cost of a significant drop in the 
saving rate (3.8% at the end of the quarter, down from 4.4% at the end of the previous quarter). The United States nonetheles s 
continues to "import its growth": net trade is weighing on GDP owing to a surge in imports (19.7% AR), driven by AI -related 
components – IT, semiconductors and telecommunications (103.8% AR).

### Page 2

Document 357: Fed Likely To Hike By 50bps In December

Source PDF: all report pdf/Fed Likely To Hike By 50bps In December.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-11
Pages: 7
Status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: Fed Likely To Hike By 50bps In December
Date:    7 hours ago
Fed Likely To Hike By 50bps In December

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
Fed Likely To Hike By 50bps In
December
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 10 Nov 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Photo by liam ward / Unsplash
I. The Fed Has More Work To Do
On October 26, we warned that the Fed could surprise markets with a
25bps rate hike at its October meeting. If the Fed chose to pass on doing
so because the midterm elections were too close, we predicted a 50bps
hike in December. Two days later, the FOMC voted 10-2 to hold the
federal funds rate (FFR) at 3.75%-4.00%, with the two dissenters favoring
a 25bps hike. Yet, in his October 28 presser, Fed Chair Kevin Warsh's

### Page 3

assessment of the economy sounded decidedly hawkish. Economic
growth was robust and broadening, the labor market was in good shape,
inflation remained above the Fed's 2% target, and financial conditions
were hardly tight.
So why did the Fed stand pat?
Warsh argued that financial conditions had tightened, inflation
expectations remained anchored, and stronger productivity growth was
allowing the economy to expand without overheating. With an information-

rich period ahead, the Fed saw little reason to rush. We called that
decision puzzling, comparing it to someone standing in a room with a fire
alarm blaring and smoke pouring under the door, yet insisting on more
evidence before calling the fire department.
Now Fed Governor Christopher Waller is sounding the same alarm.
Speaking in London on Tuesday, Waller said he would support a 25bps
rate hike in December even if inflation improved in October. However, if

October's core CPI increased by around 0.3% m/m or more, as it had in
the previous two months, he argued that a larger move would be
warranted. Specifically, he said that "a 50 basis point hike in the policy
rate should be considered at the December meeting." Waller warned that
moving by only 25bps under those circumstances would jeopardize the
Fed's inflation-fighting credibility.
Economic data released since Waller's remarks suggest that a 50bps hike

in December is becoming increasingly likely. We believe such a move is
warranted, as it would bring the FFR back to our estimated neutral range
of 4.25%-4.50%. Consider the following:
(1) CPI Inflation. October's headline CPI rose by 0.4% m/m, while core
CPI increased 0.3% m/m for the third consecutive month (chart).
Inflationary pressures remain broad-based, with particularly strong
increases in energy, food, and transportation services. Importantly, the

core CPI reading meets Waller's condition for necessitating a 50bps rate
hike in December.

### Page 4

Three forces are contributing to renewed goods inflation: the AI
investment boom, higher energy prices stemming from the Iran war, and
tariffs. In October, prices rose sharply in tariff-sensitive categories such as
furniture & bedding and appliances (chart). However, overall durable
goods prices were essentially unchanged, largely reflecting a 1.2%
decline in used car and truck prices.
Inflationary pressures were also widespread among nondurable goods,

with prices rising 0.5% m/m in October (chart). Recreational reading
materials, housekeeping supplies, footwear, and energy saw particularly
sharp increases. Apparel prices were essentially unchanged, while prices
for computer and software accessories fell.

### Page 5

Another concerning aspect in the inflation data is the persistence of
services inflation, reflecting resilient consumer demand, housing cost
pressures, and the pass-through of higher energy prices. Services prices
rose 0.4% m/m in October, including a 0.4% increase in services
excluding shelter (chart). Postage and delivery, hospital services,
transportation services, and motor vehicle insurance rose sharply.
Owners' equivalent rent also rose 0.3%.

(2) Small Business Sentiment. Echoing the CPI report's fire alarm,
October's NFIB survey showed that the net share of small business
owners planning to raise selling prices jumped to 37%, the highest since
July 2022, while 24% planned to raise worker compensation, the highest
since December 2025 (chart).

### Page 6

(3) Consumer spending. Spending remains remarkably robust. Redbook
same-store retail sales rose 10.1% y/y in the week of November 6, well
above the 2025 full-year average of 5.8% (chart).
(4) Labor market. One of the key reasons consumer spending remains
remarkably strong is the continued strength of the labor market. The latest
ADP data support this assessment. Private employers added an average
of 19,750 jobs per week during the four weeks ending October 24 (chart).

That translates into roughly 86,000 jobs per month, a healthy pace of job
creation that is sufficient to keep the unemployment rate near its current
low level of 4.1%.

### Page 7

clause.

Document 364: France_ double-digit growth in business creations, a slowdown in the rise in bankruptcies and stable non-performing loans ratio (2026-10-08)

Source PDF: all report pdf/France_ double-digit growth in business creations, a slowdown in the rise in bankruptcies and stable non-performing loans ratio (2026-10-08).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-08
Pages: 1
Status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 8 October 2026

EcoFlash

France: double-digit growth in business creations, a slowdown in the rise in bankruptcies and 
stable non-performing loans ratio

Business creations rose by 12% y/y over the first eight months of 2026 1. This is significantly higher than the 5.3% y/y 
growth recorded in 2025. This increase was driven by sectors linked to information and communication technologies.

Meanwhile, business bankruptcies rose by 5.3% y/y between January and July, according to data from the Banque de 
France (compared with the 3.6% y/y increase for the whole of 2025). The rise was more pronounced in the 1st quarter (+8% y/y) 
before slowing down. This slowdown could persist, as, according to Altares, payment delays have fallen to an average of 12.3 
days in the second quarter of 2026, compared with 14.1 days a year earlier (and 13.1 days for the European average).

Rising bankruptcies are more heavily affecting the services sector, which is also experiencing slightly stronger growth 
in business creations. This correlation is particularly noticeable in the retail sector, as business creations have risen by 13% 
since the start of 2026, while bankruptcies are up by 3%. In construction and manufacturing, business bankruptcies have been 
falling since the second quarter of 2026.

The average ratio of non -performing corporate loans has remained stable at a moderately high level for the past year  
(4.0% of total loans and advances in Q2 2026). In construction, where activity is more cyclical than in other sectors, the ra tio of 
non-performing loans has reached its highest level since 2019, standing at 9.5%.

The proportion of loans that have deteriorated but are not yet non-performing (“Stage 2” according to IFRS 9 terminology) 
is stable, standing at 15.4% q/q, and remains below its all -time high (16.1%). This indicator, which helps to anticipate trends in 
non-performing loans, is relatively encouraging.

Despite a significant increase since 2021, the economic weight of bankruptcies remains, on average, below its peak in 
Q1 2025 (0.53 compared with 0.61, respectively). This indicator generally allows bankruptcies to be weighted based on the size 
of the businesses. However, the economic weight of bankrupted small businesses is at a historically high level (2.11).

Take a look at our EcoCharts on trends in business creations and bankruptcies in France and the 
associated risks. It will be updated on a quarterly basis.

Thomas Humblot 
Economiste, team: Banking economics 
thomas.humblot@bnpparibas.com

Stéphane Colliac 
Head of Advanced economies, France 
stephane.colliac@bnpparibas.com

1 For more details, see Business creations and bankruptcies in France: double-digit growth in business creations, lower growth in bankruptcies, 
and stable non-performing loans, 8 October 2026.

Document 371: GPIF、運用姿勢に変化なし ～2026年9月末の運用実績速報、国内債券の比率引き上げ観測は後退か～

Source PDF: all report pdf/GPIF、運用姿勢に変化なし ～2026年9月末の運用実績速報、国内債券の比率引き上げ観測は後退か～.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-11-09
Pages: 3
Status: ok

### Page 1

Flash Insights

2026年 11月 9日

1/3 
GPIF、運用姿勢に変化なし 
～2026年 9月末の運用実績速報、国内債券の比率引き上げ観測は後退か～ 
 資産運用調査部 主席研究員  奥田 宏二

1. 2026年 7～9月の収益率はマイナス 1.65％ 
年金積立金管理運用独立行政法人（GPIF）は 2026年 11月6日、同年 7～9月の運用実績を公表
した。期間収益率はマイナス1.65％だった。7～9月は国内外の長期金利が上昇し、債券価格が下
落した。加えて円高も進み、外国資産の円換算額を押し下げた。資産別の収益率は国内債券がマ
イナス 1.80％、外国債券がマイナス6.52％、国内株式がプラス3.71％、外国株式がマイナス
1.86％となった。9月末の運用資産額は314兆3,876億円と6月末から約3.4兆円減少した。 
GPIFを巡っては、片山さつき財務・金融相が7月に「日本の金融資産にさらなる投資をしてい
ただくという方向で後押しをする方策を追求したい」と発言し、ポートフォリオに変化があるか
注目が集まっていた。長期金利が上昇していたこともあり、特に国内債への関心が高かった。 
結論から言うと、国内債比率は基本ポートフォリオの25％に極めて近く、運用姿勢の変化を読
み取ることは難しい。国内債の比率を引き上げるのではないかという観測は一旦後退するだろ
う。

2. 国内債券比率は 25％近辺を維持  
GPIFの基本ポートフォリオは国内外の株式と債券をそれぞれ25％とし、国内債券には±6ポイ
ントの乖離許容幅が設定されている。GPIFの公表資料によると、年金積立金全体に占める国内債
比率は 2026年9月末時点で26.49％だった。これには年金特別会計で管理する積立金、約 5.4兆
円が含まれている。 
この積立金を国内債券と年金積立金全体から除き、GPIFの運用資産ベースで計算すると、9月末
の国内債券比率は25.23％となる。6月末の24.98％から 0.25ポイント上昇したが、基本ポートフ
ォリオの25％をわずかに上回る水準だ（資料1）。過去の実績と比較しても、今回の比率は突出し
ていない。2020～2025年度の 9月末平均は 25.05％で、2026年9月末はこれを0.18ポイント上回
る程度だ。

### Page 2

2/3 
資料 1 GPIFの運用資産構成比率（推計、2026年 6月末・9月末）

（注）年金特別会計で管理する積立金を除いたGPIF運用資産ベース。 
（出所）GPIF「2026年度第 1四半期運用状況（速報）」、「同第 2四半期運用状況（速報）」より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成。

3. 国内債券の買越額は推計 1.4兆円  
次に、7～9月の国内債券への買越額を推計する。6月末と9月末の国内債券残高の変化から、
期間中の運用損益による増減を除いて推計した。 
年金特別会計で管理する積立金を除いた国内債券残高は、6月末の79兆3,840億円から9月末
の79兆3,206億円へ、634億円減少した。一方、7～9月の国内債券の運用損益はマイナス1兆
4,452億円だった。この差額から、国内債券への買越額は約1.4兆円と推計される（注 1）。4～6
月の約 5.7兆円と比較すると、国内債券への資金配分額は減少した（注2）。 
同じ方法で他の資産への資金配分を試算すると、外国債券は約3.3兆円の買い越しとなった。
一方、国内株式は約2.0兆円、外国株式は約0.8兆円の売り越しとなった。

4. 国内債券の運用姿勢に変化はみられず  
2026年7～9月の運用実績をみる限り、GPIFが国内債券の乖離許容幅を積極的に活用し、基本ポ
ートフォリオの25％を上回る水準へと比率を引き上げようとする動きは確認できなかった。 
国内債券は買い越しているとみられるものの、外国債券には国内債券を上回る資金が配分され
ている。4資産の比率はいずれも25％近い。基本ポートフォリオに近づけて運用する従来の姿勢
が維持されていると考えられる。 
以 上

### Page 3

経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。

【注釈】
1) 買越額は国内債券残高の変化から運用損益を控除した推計値。国内債券には為替ヘッジ付き外
国債券や円建て短期資産が含まれるため、日本国債の純購入額とは必ずしも一致しない。
2) 2026年7～9月は、GPIFの運用資産額の増減から運用収益額を差し引くと、約1.9兆円の資金
流入があった計算になる。4～6月はほぼゼロだった。こうした資金流入の違いも、国内債券の
買越額を比較する際には考慮する必要がある。

Document 404: NZ Q3 Labour market data - Potential RBNZ implications (2026-11-11)

Source PDF: all report pdf/NZ Q3 Labour market data - Potential RBNZ implications (2026-11-11).pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 11
Status: ok

### Page 1

#labour #newzealand
NZ Q3 Labour market data - Potential RBNZ implications
By Kelly Eckhold
Chief Economist, Westpac New Zealand
13:16 November 04 2026 Share
The Q3 labour market statistics were weaker than expected. We discuss potential
implications for the RBNZ noting that a December hike remains more likely than not at this
point.
Q3 Labour market data: potential RBNZ implications
The Q3 labour market data were weaker than RBNZ and our expectations.

### Page 2

Today's labour market data seem unequivocally weaker than expected by either us or the RBNZ. While
likely not a gamechanger for the cycle overall, it will add ammunition to the arguments of the MPC
doves that a pause in interest rate rises will make sense soon - perhaps in December or in the New
Year. It’s likely the case that a bit much was priced into December, so the change in market
probabilities in the wake of today’s data makes sense - although my base case remains that a
December rate rise remains more likely than not.
The message from the RBNZ in the October meeting though was to be careful about pricing in "done
deals" and that the outlook will be data-dependent. As we noted at the time, alongside measures of
inflation expectations, the key domestic data to watch ahead of the RBNZ’s 9 December meeting will
be indicators of what is happening to excess capacity (the output gap) as this will be a key determinant
of the Bank’s assessment of the outlook for inflation over the coming year.
It’s useful to remember in the RBNZ’s forecasting framework that GDP figures are the main
determinant of excess capacity in the economy (i.e., the output gap). The Q2 GDP data told us that the
output gap probably narrowed a bit through Q2, mainly reflecting historical revisions. In light of today’s
labour market numbers, the forecast for growth in Q3 may have some downside risk - but there are
conflicting indicators there, so it’s not that clear. However, it’s probably hard to argue that the level of
excess capacity reduced further in Q3 given today’s data. 
In the eyes of dovish MPC members, the rise in the unemployment rate and other measures important
for the RBNZ's labour market dashboard likely push in the direction of having greater comfort with the
medium-term inflation outlook. The vacancy to unemployment ratio increased a bit, labour constraints
eased a bit, and the unemployment rate has increased. Hence from a labour market perspective it
could be argued that policy could have more time all else equal. The outcome of the election, with the
possibility of a new RBNZ mandate, could increase the prominence of these factors - although this
won’t likely be an explicit factor in the RBNZ’s thinking in December even though it may be in the minds
of individual MPC members.
It could be the case that the argument for increases in the OCR track through 2027 could be
undermined with this data. It’s important to note (probably self-evidently) that there are other data that
will also be relevant. Hence the risk of a pause earlier in the New Year has probably increased with this
data, all else equal. I don't think our forecast peak OCR is really at issue here, but the time taken to get
there could be more flexible. Takes along the lines of "3% is the terminal rate" are very premature and
misplaced, in our view.

A December hike remains more likely than not, but there will be a clear debate to be had between the
MPC hawks and doves.
The forecast peak OCR is likely less impacted than the time to reach that peak. Further pauses in the
tightening cycle could come earlier than currently forecast. 
The outlook for the exchange rate is especially clouded by this data should it take a longer time for
NZ rates to move closer to global norms. 
Watch for updated RBNZ commentary on 10 and 19 November. The Governor’s observation that it
would have been useful to update markets ahead of the October meeting could imply a greater
willingness to comment in the coming weeks.

### Page 3

The outlook for the exchange rate is undermined by this data. I feel very happy about our year-end
target of 79c for the NZD/AUD rate with the risk that a recovery in the NZD takes longer than currently
assumed.
Looking ahead, there are some opportunities for the RBNZ to update markets on their views. Top of
mind is Paul Conway's 10 November speech to the UBS Australasia Conference. The Governor is also
speaking to the Institute of Directors on 19 November. If the RBNZ has something to say, then it might
happen in conjunction with these events (the 10 November speech is titled “How is the NZ economy
tracking”, so it will be difficult for Conway to avoid saying something about how he is interpreting
today’s data). We also note the Governor's comments in the October MPR that it could have been
useful to have been more flexible about updating markets on emerging views of new economic data.
This might increase the likelihood that these events could be used to provide guidance on the RBNZ’s
views of this data (and other emerging events), especially if it feels that the market’s interpretation is at
odds with its own.
